## 7. Performance budget

From the §2.0 addendum, verbatim numbers (mobile mid-tier):

- **LCP < 2.5s, INP < 200ms, CLS < 0.05.**
- **Initial JS ≤ 60 KB gzip**, excluding the lazily loaded WebGL/demo chunks.
- **WebGL starts only after first paint and on idle, only when visible, and never under reduced
  motion or Save-Data or with `deviceMemory < 4`.**
- **It pauses when offscreen or when the tab is hidden.**

#288 retired the landing's only WebGL (the constellation): no canvas loop runs on `/`, and the
spotlight (one coalesced custom-property write per frame) keeps the same pauses. The WebGL rules
above bind any future one.

Prompt-level budgets carried with it: the hero island ≤ 20 KB gzip (L2); each demo chunk ≤ 35 KB
gzip, only the active one loaded (L3); WebGL `devicePixelRatio` capped at 1.5 (L1, moot since #288);
`/features` initial JS ≤ the landing budget, demo chunks on "Try it" (L5).

**What the 60 KB counts (Decision #286 (A), ruled 2026-10-18: strip + defer + rescope).** The
addendum's 60 KB is **landing-owned** initial JS: every chunk a landing island reaches beyond the
app baseline — `LandingShell`'s header and the page's own islands (hero, theatre, background,
demos), whether or not the footer or a layout host shares the chunk. It fails over 60 KB. The **app
baseline** (the client entry and the `_app.tsx` islands, loaded by every page of the app) is tracked
apart and held to its **pin**, main's measured size, committed as
`features/landing/core/js-budget-baseline.json` (`{ bytes, measuredAt, mainSha }`) and re-pinned
after each main merge (Decision #289 (A), which replaces #286 (A) / #287 (A)(12)'s "the pin only
goes down"): **110,956 bytes** (111.0 KB), re-pinned 2026-10-20 at main `91948f22` (`a04fd582`;
before it 110,845 bytes at `20c36095`, and the first pin, 110,842 bytes at `dee9a336`). The layout
hosts and the footer islands are reported as what each adds beyond those two groups, and the page
totals for a guest and for a signed-in visitor are reported and never fail. The hero island keeps
its ≤ 20 KB own weight, and every lazy chunk the landing owns (its source under `features/landing/`,
or loaded by `import()` from a landing-owned chunk) its ≤ 35 KB. Two hosts left the landing's
initial JS to get there: `/` and `/features` strip `?tkv=` on the server with a `303`
(`features/landing/core/strip-tkv.ts`; `ROUTING.md`) instead of mounting `TicketDeepLinkHost`, and a
signed-in visitor gets `LazyChatHost`, which loads `ChatPopoutHost` only once a floating chat is
open in the tab.

**Re-pinning the app baseline (Decision #289 (A), owner ruling 2026-10-19: "Re-pin on each main
merge").** Each run compares `git merge-base HEAD main` with the pin's `mainSha`:

- **Held** — they match (no main merge since the pin): one byte over the pin fails, so landing
  commits cannot grow the baseline. A smaller baseline may lower the pin at any time.
- **Main moved** — they differ (main was merged into the branch since): the baseline is reported
  against the pin as "main moved, re-pin" and never fails. Main's growth is main's.
- **Unresolved** — the checkout has neither `main` nor `origin/main`: held.

After every merge of main into the landing branch, in the merge commit or the one right after it,
run `deno task build && deno task budget:landing --repin` and commit `js-budget-baseline.json`.
`--repin` writes the measured baseline with the new merge base and the build's time. It refuses
(exit 1) to raise the pin while main has not been merged since (that growth is the branch's own),
and (exit 2) a working tree with uncommitted changes under `apps`, `packages`, `vite.config.ts`,
`deno.json` or `deno.lock` (re-pin a committed tree only: a lazy landing chunk can move bytes into
the shared baseline chunks, so uncommitted work would be pinned as main's; #298 (G)), a build older
than HEAD, the baseline's sources or any watched landing source, or a checkout without main.
`deno task budget:landing --help` prints the same procedure.

**The build id is measured as a constant (Decision #302 (E), FLAG for the owner).** Fresh writes the
commit checked out at build time into the client entry as a 40-hex string, and gzip of 40 random hex
characters varies by up to about 11 bytes from one commit to the next (9,671–9,682 over 200 random
ids on the L5 client entry), so a held pin with no headroom passed or failed on the commit's hash:
the L5 tree read 110,847 built at HEAD `48304ff2` and 110,841 with `19b9ea0a`'s id. The script now
gzips the client entry with that id replaced by one fixed stand-in (`BUILD_ID_STANDIN`,
`core/js-budget.ts`): `1d90ea0b`'s, the id the pin's own build carried — 110,845 is exactly the
client entry gzipped with it plus the other baseline chunks, unchanged since — so every reading is
measured as the pin was. Other chunk bytes are measured as built.

**L3 review, 2026-10-19 (#298 (G)).** The build that held the whole of L3 read the app baseline at
110,891 bytes, 49 over the pin with no main merge since: the lazy demos' `platformFeeFor` import
kept that function in the shared baseline chunk that carries `packages/types/finance/checkout.ts`.
The fee moved to the leaf `packages/types/finance/fee.ts` (`@projective/types/finance/fee`,
re-exported by `checkout.ts`), which the landing imports; the baseline is back at the pin (110.8 kB,
held) with no re-pin, landing-owned 24.9 / 60 kB, DemoHost 7.3 / 8 kB own weight, every lazy demo ≤
32.5 / 35 kB.

**L4, 2026-10-19 (#301 (J)).** The build that holds L4 and its review reads landing-owned 29.2 / 60
kB — 1.1 kB of it `@projective/ui`'s MoneyView island, now counted on `/`: its island module
default-exports the same function How it works and the demo posters render, Fresh registers an
island by that identity, and `island-graph.ts` follows the binding (`islandBindingsOf`), so the
page's declared islands match what it ships — PricingCalculator 2.5 / 6 kB own weight, RailScroll
1.3 / 4 kB, HeroSearch 19.3 / 20 kB, DemoHost 7.3 / 8 kB; the app baseline 110.8 kB held at the pin
(main `20c36095`, no main merge since); page totals 149.7 kB for everyone and 153.8 kB signed in,
reported. The figures How it works and the demo posters print still hydrate as MoneyView roots; a
non-island rendering for those static figures is a follow-up.

**L5, 2026-10-20 (#302 (E), (G)).** The build that holds L5 and its review reads, the client entry
measured with the build-id stand-in: the app baseline 110,845 / 110,845 bytes on `/` and
`/features`, held at the pin (main `20c36095`, no main merge since; 110,847 as built at HEAD
`48304ff2` — the hash, not the code). `/features`: landing-owned 24.2 / 60 kB; HeroSearch 19.3 / 20
kB own weight; FeatureDemoDialog 20.4 / 22 kB own weight — its closure beyond the client entry, of
which ~16 kB is the Dialog family the baseline already loads for OfflineBridge; it adds 4.1 kB
exclusive (its chunk 2.7 kB, and the demos' loader shared with `DemoHost`, 1.4 kB). No work order
sets that cap: 22 kB is L5's reading with headroom, flagged for the owner with an exclusive-weight
cap as the alternative (§9 row 43). Its lazy demos are the stage's own chunks, each loaded only for
the pressed row: Board 14.9, Payments 4.6, Booking 32.5, Team pay 8.5 / 35 kB. Page totals 144.7 kB
for everyone and 148.8 kB signed in, reported. `/` after the shared loader: landing-owned 29.7 / 60
kB, DemoHost 7.6 / 8 kB own weight, page totals 150.1 / 154.3 kB.

**L6, 2026-10-20 (#303).** The fix pass's build reads, with the build-id stand-in: the app baseline
110,956 / 110,956 bytes on `/` and `/features`, held at the pin (main `91948f22`, no main merge
since); `/` landing-owned 29.7 / 60 kB (HeroSearch 19.3 / 20, DemoHost 7.6 / 8, HeroTheatre 3.9 /
12, PricingCalculator 2.5 / 6, RailScroll 1.3 / 4, MoneyFlow 1.0 / 8 kB own weight), page totals
150.2 / 154.4 kB; `/features` landing-owned 24.2 / 60 kB, FeatureDemoDialog 20.4 / 22 kB own weight,
page totals 144.7 / 148.9 kB; every lazy chunk ≤ 32.5 / 35 kB. Within budget. **LCP fails on the
phone, and not on the landing's bytes** (#303 FLAG (a)): measured by the method below on
`deno task serve` (which compresses nothing), `/` reads 3,548 ms median (3,796 worst, n = 15) and
`/features` 2,812 ms (n = 5), FCP equal to LCP, waiting on 170 kB of HTML and 291 kB of CSS
identity-encoded; the same build behind gzip reads 1,944 ms and 1,860 ms. INP (p98 120 / 128 ms
median) and CLS (0.00002 / 0) pass. Desktop passes either way. The final re-run on a fresh build of
the fixed tree (§10) reads the same budget (`/` landing-owned 29,671 bytes, `/features` 24,196; the
baseline 110,956 at the pin) and, on the phone as served, `/` 3,496 ms median (3,912 worst, n = 5)
and `/features` 2,688 ms; behind gzip 1,864 ms and 1,760 ms. INP (112 / 120 ms) and CLS (0.00002
/ 0) pass.

## 10. L6 launch gate (2026-10-20)

The final verification of the landing track, after the #303 fix pass, on a fresh production build:
the worktree at `a04fd582` with the fix pass applied and uncommitted (`deno task build`, exit 0,
03:19–03:21 BST), served by `deno serve -A --unstable-kv apps/web/_fresh/server.js` on a local port
(what `deno task serve` runs) under `env -u SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`, against the live local
Supabase. The in-process harnesses import the same build (1,864 files, md5-checked before and after
the run). Browser: system Chrome, headless, through `npm:playwright-core@1.56.1`; axe-core 4.10.3 is
injected by the scratch harness and is not a repo dependency. Methods are §7's. The leak check
compares against a throwaway worktree of main `91948f22` (the merge base), built for it and removed
after. Evidence paths are relative to the session scratchpad
`C:/Users/Ahmed/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/C--Users-Ahmed--claude-spares-spare-L8S9Ye/4a42c780-d0b5-4519-8891-67f470667e95/scratchpad/`:
`l6/final/` holds this run, `l6/` the audit's. Nothing is committed, so every fix below is in the
working tree with its commit pending. The focus ring is recorded and never failed (#285 (A)).

**LAUNCH VERDICT: NOT CLEARED.** The landing's own work passes: its copy is honest sentence by
sentence (§10.1), its JavaScript is within budget with the app baseline held, it is operable by
keyboard and clean under axe in every state, it shows no motion under either reduced-motion channel,
and it changes no app route's CSS. Launch is blocked by: **(1)** #303 FLAG (a), transport
compression: the throttled-phone LCP fails on both pages as served (3.5 s and 2.7 s) and passes
behind gzip (1.9 s and 1.8 s); **(2)** #303 FLAG (b), the head tags parsed in `<body>` on every page
(canonical, description, robots and social tags are invisible to crawlers that read `<head>`);
**(3)** §9 row 8, the lawyer's review of the payment-protection copy (§4.6 and the FAQ) and of P1's
draft escrow terms, which are still `reviewedAt: null`, so the terms link stays withheld (#301 (B));
**(4)** #303 FLAG (c), Y4: nothing in code keeps seeded or Stripe test-mode money out of the public
numbers, which matters the moment a deployment holds ten or more escrows. (1) and (2) are app-wide,
pre-existing and outside the landing's exempt paths; their patches are ready and each needs an
owner's or architect's OK and a decision row. Before launch, the shared database must also take the
regenerated help seed (`00005071`), or the linked help pages keep the removed absolutes. Waiting on
the owner but not blocking: #303 FLAGS (d)–(f); #301 FLAGS (a)–(k); #302 FLAGS (a)–(e), (g), (h)
((f) is closed by #303 (E)); #298 (A) and (F); og:image (§9 row 16); and the INP risk on the phone's
first keystroke recorded above.

### 10.1 The claims walk (L6 final)

Walked on the same build in-process (`l6/final/claims/walk.ts`; `sentences.ts` → `analyze.ts` over
`mapping.ts`, with §5.2's FT rows parsed beside §5's): `/` and `/features` at 1280 dark, at 375
light with touch, and without JavaScript; the four demos' reduced-motion frame walks and their
interactive passes (Fair Exit at 25, 50 and 75); seven calculator states; the hero search; the 375
menu and category sheets; the four `/features` dialogs; and every same-origin link. The audit's full
walk with its issue list is `l6/claims.md`; this is the re-run on the fixed tree.

| Measure                                                         | Value                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| :-------------------------------------------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Unique strings                                                  | 833: 317 claim-bearing, 217 labels, calls to action and controls, 168 example data, 93 live data, 38 hidden concatenations                                                                                 |
| Claim-bearing sentences without a §5 or §5.2 row                | 0 (the audit: 11, all in the footer)                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Rows rendered                                                   | 107 of 115; not rendered by design: A8 (hero option 2), K6 (needs a drag), Q1 (alternate FAQ), W1 (held, #302 (B)), Y1–Y4 (the metrics are null, so the section is hidden)                                 |
| Verdicts                                                        | 305 PASS; 8 PASS, labelled Coming soon; 3 PASS on FT2 and FT4 (#303 FLAG (e)); 1 FLAG (V9, #302 (h))                                                                                                       |
| Banned terms · "escrow" outside its slots · §5.1 omitted claims | 0 · 0 · 0                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Links                                                           | 72 same-origin links: every one answers 200, except four that send a guest to `/login` (`/projects?create=1`, `/catalogue?create=service`, `/settings`, `/settings/verification`), each route's own answer |
